<div class="col-sm order-1">
      <h2 class="chapter_title">CHAPTER 245A. HUMAN SERVICES LICENSING</h2>
      
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245A.001"><b>245A.001</b> MS 2006 [Renumbered 15.001]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245A.01">
        <h1 class="shn">245A.01 CITATION.</h1>
        <p>This chapter may be cited as the "Human Services Licensing Act."</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245A.01.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/333/#laws.0.1.0">1987 c 333 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/327/#laws.0.6.0">2000 c 327 s 6</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245A.02">
        <h1 class="shn">245A.02 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope.</span></h2>
          <p>The terms used in this chapter have the meanings given them in this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Adult.</span></h2>
          <p>"Adult" means a person who is 18 years old or older and who:</p>
          <p>(1) has mental illness, developmental disability, physical disability, or functional impairment; or</p>
          <p>(2) has substance use disorder or abuses substances.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Adult day care or family adult day services.</span></h2>
          <p>"Adult day care," "adult day services," and "family adult day services" mean a program operating less than 24 hours per day that provides functionally impaired adults with an individualized and coordinated set of services including health services, social services, and nutritional services that are directed at maintaining or improving the participants' capabilities for self-care. Adult day care, adult day services, and family adult day services do not include programs where adults gather or congregate primarily for purposes of socialization, education, supervision, caregiver respite, religious expression, exercise, or nutritious meals.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.2b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2b.<span class="headnote">Annual or annually.</span></h2>
          <p>With the exception of subdivision 2c, "annual" or "annually" means prior to or within the same month of the subsequent calendar year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.2c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2c.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142B.01">142B.01, subd 3</a>] </p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Applicant.</span></h2>
          <p>"Applicant" means an individual, organization, or government entity, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.02#stat.13.02.7a">13.02, subdivision 7a</a>, that is subject to licensure under this chapter and that has applied for but not yet been granted a license under this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.3a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Certification.</span></h2>
          <p>"Certification" means the commissioner's written authorization for a license holder licensed by the commissioner of human services or the commissioner of corrections to serve children in a residential program and provide specialized services based on certification standards in Minnesota Rules. The term "certification" and its derivatives have the same meaning and may be substituted for the term "licensure" and its derivatives in this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.3b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3b.<span class="headnote">Authorized agent.</span></h2>
          <p>"Authorized agent" means the controlling individual designated by the license holder responsible for communicating with the commissioner of human services on all matters related to this chapter and on whom service of all notices and orders must be made pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.04#stat.245A.04.1">245A.04, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.3c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3c.<span class="headnote">At risk of becoming a victim of sex trafficking or commercial sexual exploitation.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.25">245A.25</a>, a youth who is "at risk of becoming a victim of sex trafficking or commercial sexual exploitation" means a youth who meets the criteria established by the commissioner of human services for this purpose.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Child.</span></h2>
          <p>"Child" means a person who has not reached age 18.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.4a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Children's residential facility.</span></h2>
          <p>"Children's residential facility" means a residential program licensed under this chapter or chapter 241 according to the applicable standards in Minnesota Rules, parts <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/2960.0010">2960.0010</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/2960.0710">2960.0710</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p>"Commissioner" means the commissioner of human services or the commissioner's designated representative including county agencies and private agencies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.5a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5a.<span class="headnote">Controlling individual.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Controlling individual" means an owner of a program or service provider licensed under this chapter and the following individuals, if applicable:</p>
          <p>(1) each officer of the organization, including the chief executive officer and chief financial officer;</p>
          <p>(2) the individual designated as the authorized agent under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.04">245A.04, subdivision </a>1, paragraph (b);</p>
          <p>(3) the individual designated as the compliance officer under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.04">256B.04, subdivision </a>21, paragraph (g);</p>
          <p>(4) each managerial official whose responsibilities include the direction of the management or policies of a program; and</p>
          <p>(5) the president and treasurer of the board of directors of a nonprofit corporation.</p>
          <p>(b) Controlling individual does not include:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) a bank, savings bank, trust company, savings association, credit union, industrial loan and thrift company, investment banking firm, or insurance company unless the entity operates a program directly or through a subsidiary;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) an individual who is a state or federal official, or state or federal employee, or a member or employee of the governing body of a political subdivision of the state or federal government that operates one or more programs, unless the individual is also an officer, owner, or managerial official of the program, receives remuneration from the program, or owns any of the beneficial interests not excluded in this subdivision;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) an individual who owns less than five percent of the outstanding common shares of a corporation:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) whose securities are exempt under section 80A.45, clause (6); or</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) whose transactions are exempt under section 80A.46, clause (2);</p>
          <p class="in">(4) an individual who is a member of an organization exempt from taxation under section 290.05, unless the individual is also an officer, owner, or managerial official of the program or owns any of the beneficial interests not excluded in this subdivision. This clause does not exclude from the definition of controlling individual an organization that is exempt from taxation; or</p>
          <p>(5) an employee stock ownership plan trust, or a participant or board member of an employee stock ownership plan, unless the participant or board member is a controlling individual according to paragraph (a).</p>
          <p>(c) For purposes of this subdivision, "managerial official" means an individual who has the decision-making authority related to the operation of the program, and the responsibility for the ongoing management of or direction of the policies, services, or employees of the program. A site director who has no ownership interest in the program is not considered to be a managerial official for purposes of this definition.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.5b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5b.<span class="headnote">Cradleboard.</span></h2>
          <p>"Cradleboard" means a board or frame on which an infant is secured using blankets or other material, such as fabric or leather sides, and laces and often has a frame extending to protect the infant's head. The infant is always placed with the infant's head facing outward, and the infant remains supervised in the cradleboard while sleeping or being carried.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">County agency.</span></h2>
          <p>"County agency" means the agency designated by the county board of commissioners, human service boards, local social services agencies or multicounty local social services agencies, or departments where those have been established under the law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.6a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6a.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142B.01">142B.01, subd 11</a>] </p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.6b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6b.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142B.01">142B.01, subd 12</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.6c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6c.<span class="headnote">Foster care for adults.</span></h2>
          <p>"Foster care for adults" means a program operating 24 hours a day that provides functionally impaired adults with food, lodging, protection, supervision, and household services in a residence, in addition to services according to the individual service plans under Minnesota Rules, part <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/9555.5105">9555.5105</a>, subpart 18.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.6d">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6d.<span class="headnote">Foster family setting.</span></h2>
          <p>"Foster family setting" has the meaning given in Minnesota Rules, part <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/2960.3010">2960.3010</a>, subpart 23, and includes settings licensed by the commissioner of human services or the commissioner of corrections.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.6e">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6e.<span class="headnote">Foster residence setting.</span></h2>
          <p>"Foster residence setting" has the meaning given in Minnesota Rules, part <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/2960.3010">2960.3010</a>, subpart 26, and includes settings licensed by the commissioner of human services or the commissioner of corrections.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.6f">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6f.<span class="headnote">Family adult foster care home.</span></h2>
          <p>"Family adult foster care home" means an adult foster care home:</p>
          <p>(1) that is licensed by the Department of Human Services;</p>
          <p>(2) that is the primary residence of the license holder; and</p>
          <p>(3) in which the license holder is the primary caregiver.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Functional impairment.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of adult day care, adult day services, family adult day services, or adult foster care, "functional impairment" means:</p>
          <p>(1) a condition that is characterized by substantial difficulty in carrying out one or more of the essential major activities of daily living, such as caring for oneself, performing manual tasks, walking, seeing, hearing, speaking, breathing, learning, working; or</p>
          <p>(2) a disorder of thought or mood that significantly impairs judgment, behavior, capacity to recognize reality, or ability to cope with the ordinary demands of life and that requires support to maintain independence in the community.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.7a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7a.<span class="headnote">HIV minimum standards.</span></h2>
          <p>"HIV minimum standards" means those items approved by the department and contained in the HIV-1 Guidelines for substance use disorder treatment and care programs in Minnesota including HIV education to clients, completion of HIV training by all new and existing staff, provision for referral to individual HIV counseling and services for all clients, and the implementation of written policies and procedures for working with HIV-infected clients.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.7b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7b.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/262/#laws.5.7.0">2014 c 262 art 5 s 7</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">License.</span></h2>
          <p>"License" means a certificate issued by the commissioner under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.04">245A.04 </a>authorizing the license holder to provide a specified program for a specified period of time and in accordance with the terms of the license and the rules of the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">License holder.</span></h2>
          <p>"License holder" means an individual, organization, or government entity that is legally responsible for the operation of the program or service, and has been granted a license by the commissioner under this chapter and the rules of the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Nonresidential program.</span></h2>
          <p>"Nonresidential program" means care, supervision, rehabilitation, training or habilitation of a person provided outside the person's own home and provided for fewer than 24 hours a day, including adult day care programs; and substance use disorder or substance abuse programs that are located in a nursing home or hospital and receive public funds for providing substance abuse or substance use disorder treatment services under chapter 254B. Nonresidential programs include home and community-based services for persons with disabilities or persons age 65 and older that are provided in or outside of a person's own home under chapter 245D.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.10a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10a.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142B.01">142B.01, subd 21</a>] </p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.10b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10b.<span class="headnote">Owner.</span></h2>
          <p>"Owner" means an individual or organization that has a direct or indirect ownership interest of five percent or more in a program licensed under this chapter. For purposes of this subdivision, "direct ownership interest" means the possession of equity in capital, stock, or profits of an organization, and "indirect ownership interest" means a direct ownership interest in an entity that has a direct or indirect ownership interest in a licensed program. For purposes of this chapter, "owner of an employee stock ownership plan" means the president and treasurer of the entity. A government entity or nonprofit corporation that is issued a license under this chapter shall be designated the owner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.10c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10c.<span class="headnote">Organization.</span></h2>
          <p>"Organization" means a domestic or foreign corporation, nonprofit corporation, limited liability company, partnership, limited partnership, limited liability partnership, association, voluntary association, and any other legal or commercial entity. For purposes of this chapter, organization does not include a government entity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Person.</span></h2>
          <p>"Person" means a child or adult as defined in subdivisions 2 and 4.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142B.01">142B.01, subd 22</a>] </p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Individual who is related.</span></h2>
          <p>"Individual who is related" means a spouse, a parent, a birth or adopted child or stepchild, a stepparent, a stepbrother, a stepsister, a niece, a nephew, an adoptive parent, a grandparent, a sibling, an aunt, an uncle, or a legal guardian.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Residential program.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Except as provided in paragraph (b), "residential program" means a program that provides 24-hour-a-day care, supervision, food, lodging, rehabilitation, training, education, habilitation, or treatment outside a person's own home, including a program in an intermediate care facility for four or more persons with developmental disabilities; and substance use disorder or substance abuse programs that are located in a hospital or nursing home and receive public funds for providing substance abuse or substance use disorder treatment services under chapter 254B.</p>
          <p>(b) For a residential program under chapter 245D, "residential program" means a single or multifamily dwelling that is under the control, either directly or indirectly, of the service provider licensed under chapter 245D and in which at least one person receives services under chapter 245D, including residential supports and services under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245D.03">245D.03</a>, subdivision 1, paragraph (c), clause (3); out-of-home crisis respite services under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245D.03#stat.245D.03.1">245D.03, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (c), clause (1), item (ii); and out-of-home respite services under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245D.03#stat.245D.03.1">245D.03, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b), clause (1). A residential program does not include out-of-home respite services when a case manager has determined that an unlicensed site meets the assessed needs of the person. A residential program also does not include multifamily dwellings where persons receive integrated community supports, even if authorization to provide these supports is granted under chapter 245D and approved in the federal waiver.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Respite care services.</span></h2>
          <p>"Respite care services" means temporary services provided to a person due to the absence or need for relief of the primary caregiver, the person's family member, or legal representative who is the primary caregiver and principally responsible for the care and supervision of the person. Respite care services are those that provide the level of supervision and care that is necessary to ensure the health and safety of the person. Respite care services do not include services that are specifically directed toward the training and habilitation of the person.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.16">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142B.01">142B.01, subd 25</a>] </p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.17">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142B.01">142B.01, subd 26</a>] </p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.18">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142B.01">142B.01, subd 27</a>] </p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.18a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18a.<span class="headnote">Trauma.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.25">245A.25</a>, "trauma" means an event, series of events, or set of circumstances experienced by an individual as physically or emotionally harmful or life-threatening and has lasting adverse effects on the individual's functioning and mental, physical, social, emotional, or spiritual well-being. Trauma includes the cumulative emotional or psychological harm of group traumatic experiences transmitted across generations within a community that are often associated with racial and ethnic population groups that have suffered major intergenerational losses.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.19">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 19.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142B.01">142B.01, subd 13</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.20">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 20.<span class="headnote">Weekly.</span></h2>
          <p>"Weekly" means at least once every calendar week, for the purposes of substance use disorder treatment programs licensed under chapter <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245G">245G</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.21">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 21.<span class="headnote">Monthly.</span></h2>
          <p>"Monthly" means at least once every calendar month, for the purposes of substance use disorder treatment programs licensed under chapter <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245G">245G</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.22">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 22.<span class="headnote">Quarterly.</span></h2>
          <p>"Quarterly" means at least every 90 calendar days, for the purposes of substance use disorder treatment programs licensed under chapter <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245G">245G</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.23">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 23.<span class="headnote">Victim of sex trafficking or commercial sexual exploitation.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.25">245A.25</a>, "victim of sex trafficking or commercial sexual exploitation" means a person who meets the definitions in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260C.007#stat.260C.007.31">260C.007, subdivision 31</a>, clauses (4) and (5).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.02.24">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 24.<span class="headnote">Youth.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.25">245A.25</a>, "youth" means a child as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260C.007#stat.260C.007.4">260C.007, subdivision 4</a>, and includes individuals under 21 years of age who are in foster care pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260C.451">260C.451</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245A.02.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/333/#laws.0.2.0">1987 c 333 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/411/#laws.0.1.0">1988 c 411 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/282/#laws.2.60.0">1989 c 282 art 2 s 60</a>-65; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/568/#laws.2.40.0">1990 c 568 art 2 s 40</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/142/#laws.0.1.0">1991 c 142 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/513/#laws.9.7.0">1992 c 513 art 9 s 7</a>,8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/338/#laws.0.1.0">1993 c 338 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/631/#laws.0.31.0">1994 c 631 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/158/#laws.0.1.0">1995 c 158 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/202/#laws.1.25.0">1995 c 202 art 1 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/207/#laws.2.4.0">1995 c 207 art 2 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/248/#laws.0.3.0">1997 c 248 s 3</a>-7; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/36/#laws.0.1.0">1999 c 36 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/327/#laws.0.6.0">2000 c 327 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/9/#laws.14.4.0">1Sp2001 c 9 art 14 s 4</a>-6; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/375/#laws.1.5.0">2002 c 375 art 1 s 5</a>,6; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/379/#laws.1.113.0">2002 c 379 art 1 s 113</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.2.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 2</a>-7; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/56/#laws.0.1.0">2005 c 56 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/4/#laws.1.4.0">1Sp2005 c 4 art 1 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/196/#laws.1.52.0">2006 c 196 art 1 s 52; art 2 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/108/#laws.8.8.0">2013 c 108 art 8 s 8</a>,9; art 9 s 5,6; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/228/#laws.2.2.0">2014 c 228 art 2 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/291/#laws.1.1.0">2014 c 291 art 1 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/78/#laws.4.6.0">2015 c 78 art 4 s 6</a>-9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2016/0/158/#laws.1.88.0">2016 c 158 art 1 s 88</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/6/#laws.9.1.0">1Sp2017 c 6 art 9 s 1</a>,2; art 13 s 2,3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/182/#laws.2.7.0">2018 c 182 art 2 s 7</a>-9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/9/#laws.2.14.0">1Sp2019 c 9 art 2 s 14</a>-21; art 7 s 5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/2/#laws.1.5.0">1Sp2020 c 2 art 1 s 5</a>,6; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/0/30/#laws.10.2.0">2021 c 30 art 10 s 2</a>-8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/7/#laws.13.4.0">1Sp2021 c 7 art 13 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/98/#laws.4.51.0">2022 c 98 art 4 s 51</a>; art 12 s 1; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/25/#laws.0.121.0">2023 c 25 s 121</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.8.3.0">2023 c 70 art 8 s 3</a>-5; art 17 s 9,10; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/80/#laws.2.32.0">2024 c 80 art 2 s 32</a>,33; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/115/#laws.16.47.0">2024 c 115 art 16 s 47;</a> art 19 s 3</p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245A.023"><b>245A.023</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/112/#laws.0.59.0">2007 c 112 s 59</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245A.03">
        <h1 class="shn">245A.03 WHO MUST BE LICENSED.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.03.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">License required.</span></h2>
          <p>Unless licensed by the commissioner under this chapter, an individual, organization, or government entity must not:</p>
          <p>(1) operate a residential or a nonresidential program;</p>
          <p>(2) receive a child or adult for care, supervision, or placement in foster care; or</p>
          <p>(3) advertise a residential or nonresidential program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.03.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Exclusion from licensure.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) This chapter does not apply to:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) residential or nonresidential programs that are provided to a person by an individual who is related;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) nonresidential programs that are provided by an unrelated individual to persons from a single related family;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) residential or nonresidential programs that are provided to adults who do not misuse substances or have a substance use disorder, a mental illness, a developmental disability, a functional impairment, or a physical disability;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) sheltered workshops or work activity programs that are certified by the commissioner of employment and economic development;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) programs operated by a public school for children 33 months or older;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) nonresidential programs primarily for children that provide care or supervision for periods of less than three hours a day while the child's parent or legal guardian is in the same building as the nonresidential program or present within another building that is directly contiguous to the building in which the nonresidential program is located;</p>
          <p class="in">(7) nursing homes or hospitals licensed by the commissioner of health except as specified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.02">245A.02</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(8) board and lodge facilities licensed by the commissioner of health that do not provide children's residential services under Minnesota Rules, chapter 2960, mental health or substance use disorder treatment;</p>
          <p class="in">(9) programs licensed by the commissioner of corrections;</p>
          <p class="in">(10) recreation programs for children or adults that are operated or approved by a park and recreation board whose primary purpose is to provide social and recreational activities;</p>
          <p class="in">(11) noncertified boarding care homes unless they provide services for five or more persons whose primary diagnosis is mental illness or a developmental disability;</p>
          <p class="in">(12) programs for children such as scouting, boys clubs, girls clubs, and sports and art programs, and nonresidential programs for children provided for a cumulative total of less than 30 days in any 12-month period;</p>
          <p class="in">(13) residential programs for persons with mental illness, that are located in hospitals;</p>
          <p class="in">(14) camps licensed by the commissioner of health under Minnesota Rules, chapter 4630;</p>
          <p class="in">(15) mental health outpatient services for adults with mental illness or children with emotional disturbance;</p>
          <p class="in">(16) residential programs serving school-age children whose sole purpose is cultural or educational exchange, until the commissioner adopts appropriate rules;</p>
          <p class="in">(17) community support services programs as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.462#stat.245.462.6">245.462, subdivision 6</a>, and family community support services as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.4871#stat.245.4871.17">245.4871, subdivision 17</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(18) assisted living facilities licensed by the commissioner of health under chapter 144G;</p>
          <p class="in">(19) substance use disorder treatment activities of licensed professionals in private practice as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245G.01#stat.245G.01.17">245G.01, subdivision 17</a>;</p>
          <p>(20) consumer-directed community support service funded under the Medicaid waiver for persons with developmental disabilities when the individual who provided the service is:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) the same individual who is the direct payee of these specific waiver funds or paid by a fiscal agent, fiscal intermediary, or employer of record; and</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) not otherwise under the control of a residential or nonresidential program that is required to be licensed under this chapter when providing the service;</p>
          <p>(21) a county that is an eligible vendor under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/254B.05">254B.05</a> to provide care coordination and comprehensive assessment services;</p>
          <p>(22) a recovery community organization that is an eligible vendor under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/254B.05">254B.05 </a>to provide peer recovery support services; or</p>
          <p>(23) programs licensed by the commissioner of children, youth, and families in chapter 142B.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) For purposes of paragraph (a), clause (6), a building is directly contiguous to a building in which a nonresidential program is located if it shares a common wall with the building in which the nonresidential program is located or is attached to that building by skyway, tunnel, atrium, or common roof.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) Except for the home and community-based services identified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245D.03#stat.245D.03.1">245D.03, subdivision 1</a>, nothing in this chapter shall be construed to require licensure for any services provided and funded according to an approved federal waiver plan where licensure is specifically identified as not being a condition for the services and funding.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.03.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.</h2>
          <p class="in">[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142B.05">142B.05, subd 3</a>] </p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.03.2b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2b.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142B.05">142B.05, subd 4</a>] </p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.03.2c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2c.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/37/#laws.0.3.0">2015 c 37 s 3</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.03.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Unlicensed programs.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) It is a misdemeanor for an individual, organization, or government entity to provide a residential or nonresidential program without a license issued under this chapter and in willful disregard of this chapter unless the program is excluded from licensure under subdivision 2.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner may ask the appropriate county attorney or the attorney general to begin proceedings to secure a court order against the continued operation of the program, if an individual, organization, or government entity has:</p>
          <p>(1) failed to apply for a license under this chapter after receiving notice that a license is required or continues to operate without a license after receiving notice that a license is required;</p>
          <p>(2) continued to operate without a license after a license issued under this chapter has been revoked or suspended under this chapter, and the commissioner has issued a final order affirming the revocation or suspension, or the license holder did not timely appeal the sanction; or</p>
          <p>(3) continued to operate without a license after a temporary immediate suspension of a license has been issued under this chapter.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The county attorney and the attorney general have a duty to cooperate with the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.03.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142B.05">142B.05, subd 6</a>] </p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.03.4a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142B.05">142B.05, subd 7</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.03.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>MS 2020 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/98/#laws.7.32.0">2022 c 98 art 7 s 32</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.03.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Right to seek certification.</span></h2>
          <p>Nothing in this section shall prohibit a residential program licensed by the commissioner of corrections to serve children, that is excluded from licensure under subdivision 2, paragraph (a), clause (10), from seeking certification from the commissioner of human services under this chapter for program services for which certification standards have been adopted.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.03.6a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6a.<span class="headnote">Adult foster care homes or community residential settings serving people with mental illness; certification.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner of human services shall issue a mental health certification for adult foster care homes licensed under this chapter and Minnesota Rules, parts <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/9555.5105">9555.5105</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/9555.6265">9555.6265</a>, or community residential settings licensed under chapter 245D, that serve people with a primary diagnosis of mental illness where the home is not the primary residence of the license holder when a provider is determined to have met the requirements under paragraph (b). This certification is voluntary for license holders. The certification shall be printed on the license, and identified on the commissioner's public website.</p>
          <p>(b) The requirements for certification are:</p>
          <p>(1) all staff working in the adult foster care home or community residential setting have received at least seven hours of annual training under paragraph (c) covering all of the following topics:</p>
          <p>(i) mental health diagnoses;</p>
          <p>(ii) mental health crisis response and de-escalation techniques;</p>
          <p>(iii) recovery from mental illness;</p>
          <p>(iv) treatment options including evidence-based practices;</p>
          <p>(v) medications and their side effects;</p>
          <p>(vi) suicide intervention, identifying suicide warning signs, and appropriate responses;</p>
          <p>(vii) co-occurring substance abuse and health conditions; and</p>
          <p>(viii) community resources;</p>
          <p>(2) a mental health professional, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.462#stat.245.462.18">245.462, subdivision 18</a>, or a mental health practitioner as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.462#stat.245.462.17">245.462, subdivision 17</a>, are available for consultation and assistance;</p>
          <p>(3) there is a protocol in place to address a mental health crisis; and</p>
          <p>(4) there is a crisis plan for each individual that identifies who is providing clinical services and their contact information, and includes an individual crisis prevention and management plan developed with the individual.</p>
          <p>(c) The training curriculum must be approved by the commissioner of human services and must include a testing component after training is completed. Training must be provided by a mental health professional or a mental health practitioner. Training may also be provided by an individual living with a mental illness or a family member of such an individual, who is from a nonprofit organization with a history of providing educational classes on mental illnesses approved by the Department of Human Services to deliver mental health training. Staff must receive three hours of training in the areas specified in paragraph (b), clause (1), items (i) and (ii), prior to working alone with residents. The remaining hours of mandatory training, including a review of the information in paragraph (b), clause (1), item (ii), must be completed within six months of the hire date. For programs licensed under chapter 245D, training under this section may be incorporated into the 30 hours of staff orientation required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245D.09#stat.245D.09.4">245D.09, subdivision 4</a>.</p>
          <p>(d) License holders seeking certification under this subdivision must request this certification on forms provided by the commissioner and must submit the request to the county licensing agency in which the home or community residential setting is located. The county licensing agency must forward the request to the commissioner with a county recommendation regarding whether the commissioner should issue the certification.</p>
          <p>(e) Ongoing compliance with the certification requirements under paragraph (b) shall be reviewed by the county licensing agency at each licensing review. When a county licensing agency determines that the requirements of paragraph (b) are not met, the county shall inform the commissioner, and the commissioner will remove the certification.</p>
          <p>(f) A denial of the certification or the removal of the certification based on a determination that the requirements under paragraph (b) have not been met by the adult foster care or community residential setting license holder are not subject to appeal. A license holder that has been denied a certification or that has had a certification removed may again request certification when the license holder is in compliance with the requirements of paragraph (b).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.03.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Licensing moratorium.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall not issue an initial license for child foster care licensed under Minnesota Rules, parts <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/2960.3000">2960.3000</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/2960.3340">2960.3340</a>, which does not include child foster residence settings with residential program certifications for compliance with the Family First Prevention Services Act under section 245A.25, subdivision 1, paragraph (a), or adult foster care licensed under Minnesota Rules, parts <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/9555.5105">9555.5105</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/9555.6265">9555.6265</a>, under this chapter for a physical location that will not be the primary residence of the license holder for the entire period of licensure. If a child foster residence setting that was previously exempt from the licensing moratorium under this paragraph has its Family First Prevention Services Act certification rescinded under section 245A.25, subdivision 9, or if a family adult foster care home license is issued during this moratorium, and the license holder changes the license holder's primary residence away from the physical location of the foster care license, the commissioner shall revoke the license according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.07">245A.07</a>. The commissioner shall not issue an initial license for a community residential setting licensed under chapter 245D. When approving an exception under this paragraph, the commissioner shall consider the resource need determination process in paragraph (h), the availability of foster care licensed beds in the geographic area in which the licensee seeks to operate, the results of a person's choices during their annual assessment and service plan review, and the recommendation of the local county board. The determination by the commissioner is final and not subject to appeal. Exceptions to the moratorium include:</p>
          <p>(1) a license for a person in a foster care setting that is not the primary residence of the license holder and where at least 80 percent of the residents are 55 years of age or older;</p>
          <p>(2) foster care licenses replacing foster care licenses in existence on May 15, 2009, or community residential setting licenses replacing adult foster care licenses in existence on December 31, 2013, and determined to be needed by the commissioner under paragraph (b);</p>
          <p>(3) new foster care licenses or community residential setting licenses determined to be needed by the commissioner under paragraph (b) for the closure of a nursing facility, ICF/DD, or regional treatment center; restructuring of state-operated services that limits the capacity of state-operated facilities; or allowing movement to the community for people who no longer require the level of care provided in state-operated facilities as provided under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.092">256B.092</a>, subdivision 13, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.49#stat.256B.49.24">256B.49, subdivision 24</a>;</p>
          <p>(4) new foster care licenses or community residential setting licenses determined to be needed by the commissioner under paragraph (b) for persons requiring hospital-level care; or</p>
          <p>(5) new community residential setting licenses determined necessary by the commissioner for people affected by the closure of homes with a capacity of five or six beds currently licensed as supervised living facilities licensed under Minnesota Rules, chapter 4665, but not designated as intermediate care facilities. This exception is available until June 30, 2025.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner shall determine the need for newly licensed foster care homes or community residential settings as defined under this subdivision. As part of the determination, the commissioner shall consider the availability of foster care capacity in the area in which the licensee seeks to operate, and the recommendation of the local county board. The determination by the commissioner must be final. A determination of need is not required for a change in ownership at the same address.</p>
          <p>(c) When an adult resident served by the program moves out of a foster home that is not the primary residence of the license holder according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.49#stat.256B.49.15">256B.49, subdivision 15</a>, paragraph (f), or the adult community residential setting, the county shall immediately inform the Department of Human Services Licensing Division. The department may decrease the statewide licensed capacity for adult foster care settings.</p>
          <p>(d) Residential settings that would otherwise be subject to the decreased license capacity established in paragraph (c) must be exempt if the license holder's beds are occupied by residents whose primary diagnosis is mental illness and the license holder is certified under the requirements in subdivision 6a or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245D.33">245D.33</a>.</p>
          <p>(e) A resource need determination process, managed at the state level, using the available data required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144A.351">144A.351</a>, and other data and information must be used to determine where the reduced capacity determined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.493">256B.493</a> will be implemented. The commissioner shall consult with the stakeholders described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144A.351">144A.351</a>, and employ a variety of methods to improve the state's capacity to meet the informed decisions of those people who want to move out of corporate foster care or community residential settings, long-term service needs within budgetary limits, including seeking proposals from service providers or lead agencies to change service type, capacity, or location to improve services, increase the independence of residents, and better meet needs identified by the long-term services and supports reports and statewide data and information.</p>
          <p>(f) At the time of application and reapplication for licensure, the applicant and the license holder that are subject to the moratorium or an exclusion established in paragraph (a) are required to inform the commissioner whether the physical location where the foster care will be provided is or will be the primary residence of the license holder for the entire period of licensure. If the primary residence of the applicant or license holder changes, the applicant or license holder must notify the commissioner immediately. The commissioner shall print on the foster care license certificate whether or not the physical location is the primary residence of the license holder.</p>
          <p>(g) License holders of foster care homes identified under paragraph (f) that are not the primary residence of the license holder and that also provide services in the foster care home that are covered by a federally approved home and community-based services waiver, as authorized under chapter 256S or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.092">256B.092</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.49">256B.49</a>, must inform the human services licensing division that the license holder provides or intends to provide these waiver-funded services.</p>
          <p>(h) The commissioner may adjust capacity to address needs identified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144A.351">144A.351</a>. Under this authority, the commissioner may approve new licensed settings or delicense existing settings. Delicensing of settings will be accomplished through a process identified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.493">256B.493</a>.</p>
          <p>(i) The commissioner must notify a license holder when its corporate foster care or community residential setting licensed beds are reduced under this section. The notice of reduction of licensed beds must be in writing and delivered to the license holder by certified mail or personal service. The notice must state why the licensed beds are reduced and must inform the license holder of its right to request reconsideration by the commissioner. The license holder's request for reconsideration must be in writing. If mailed, the request for reconsideration must be postmarked and sent to the commissioner within 20 calendar days after the license holder's receipt of the notice of reduction of licensed beds. If a request for reconsideration is made by personal service, it must be received by the commissioner within 20 calendar days after the license holder's receipt of the notice of reduction of licensed beds.</p>
          <p>(j) The commissioner shall not issue an initial license for children's residential treatment services licensed under Minnesota Rules, parts <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/2960.0580">2960.0580</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/2960.0700">2960.0700</a>, under this chapter for a program that Centers for Medicare and Medicaid Services would consider an institution for mental diseases. Facilities that serve only private pay clients are exempt from the moratorium described in this paragraph. The commissioner has the authority to manage existing statewide capacity for children's residential treatment services subject to the moratorium under this paragraph and may issue an initial license for such facilities if the initial license would not increase the statewide capacity for children's residential treatment services subject to the moratorium under this paragraph.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.03.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.</h2>
          <p class="in">[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142B.05">142B.05, subd 8</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.03.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Permitted services by an individual who is related.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding subdivision 2, paragraph (a), clause (1), and subdivision 7, an individual who is related to a person receiving supported living services may provide licensed services to that person if:</p>
          <p>(1) the person who receives supported living services received these services in a residential site on July 1, 2005;</p>
          <p>(2) the services under clause (1) were provided in a corporate foster care setting for adults and were funded by the developmental disabilities home and community-based services waiver defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.092">256B.092</a>;</p>
          <p>(3) the individual who is related obtains and maintains both a license under chapter 245D or successor licensing requirements for the provision of supported living services and an adult foster care license under Minnesota Rules, parts <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/9555.5105">9555.5105</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/9555.6265">9555.6265</a>; and</p>
          <p>(4) the individual who is related is not the guardian of the person receiving supported living services.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245A.03.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/333/#laws.0.3.0">1987 c 333 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/411/#laws.0.2.0">1988 c 411 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/282/#laws.2.66.0">1989 c 282 art 2 s 66</a>-68; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/568/#laws.2.41.0">1990 c 568 art 2 s 41</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/265/#laws.9.63.0">1991 c 265 art 9 s 63</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/499/#laws.3.12.0">1992 c 499 art 3 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/513/#laws.9.9.0">1992 c 513 art 9 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/338/#laws.0.3.0">1993 c 338 s 3</a>,4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/339/#laws.0.5.0">1993 c 339 s 5</a>;       <div class="sr_by_subd" id="stat.245A.035">
        <h1>245A.035</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.035.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142B.06#stat.142B.06.1">142B.06, subdivision 1</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.035.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142B.06">142B.06, subd 2]</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.035.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142B.06">142B.06, subd 3</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.035.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142B.06">142B.06, subd 4</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.035.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142B.06">142B.06, subd 5</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245A.035.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed by amendment, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/147/#laws.3.1.0">2007 c 147 art 3 s 1</a>]</p>
